In both `component_authentication.xml` and `component_password.xml`, only `passwordEdit` lives inside the `TextInputLayout`; `passwordConfirm` is a sibling `EditText` outside it. So the built-in toggle could never reach the confirmation field.\n\n`IntroScreenActivity` had already worked around this (the fix for #580) by overriding the end-icon click listener and fanning the change out to both fields — but that fix was written inline in the intro fragment, so settings never got it.\n\n## The fix\n\n**1. `Utilities/UIHelper.java`** — new shared helper that owns the behavior:\n\n```java\npublic static void setupPasswordVisibilityToggle(final TextInputLayout passwordLayout,\n                                                 final EditText passwordInput,\n                                                 final EditText passwordConfirm) {\n    passwordLayout.setEndIconOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {\n        @Override\n        public void onClick(View v) {\n            boolean wasHidden = passwordInput.getTransformationMethod() instanceof PasswordTransformationMethod;\n\n            // Dispatch the visibility change to both the password and the confirmation input\n            dispatchPasswordVisibilityChange(passwordInput, wasHidden);\n            dispatchPasswordVisibilityChange(passwordConfirm, wasHidden);\n\n            passwordLayout.refreshDrawableState();\n        }\n    });\n\n    passwordInput.setTransformationMethod(PasswordTransformationMethod.getInstance());\n    passwordConfirm.setTransformationMethod(PasswordTransformationMethod.getInstance());\n}\n```\n\n`dispatchPasswordVisibilityChange` saves/restores `getSelectionEnd()`, since swapping the transformation method resets the cursor.\n\n**2. `Preferences/CredentialsPreference.java`** (the reported bug — Settings → Authentication).
